\u003ccode\u003eq\u003c/code\u003e parameter is declared with \u003ccode\u003e= None\u003c/code\u003e, it is optional.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWithout the \u003ccode\u003eNone\u003c/code\u003e it would be required (as is the body in the case with \u003ccode\u003ePUT\u003c/code\u003e).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor \u003ccode\u003ePUT\u003c/code\u003e requests to \u003ccode\u003e/items/{item_id}\u003c/code\u003e, read the body as JSON:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCheck that it has a required attribute \u003ccode\u003ename\u003c/code\u003e that should be a \u003ccode\u003estr\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCheck that it has a required attribute \u003ccode\u003eprice\u003c/code\u003e that has to be a \u003ccode\u003efloat\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCheck that it has an optional attribute \u003ccode\u003eis_offer\u003c/code\u003e, that should be a \u003ccode\u003ebool\u003c/code\u003e, if present.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAll this would also work for deeply nested JSON objects.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eConvert from and to JSON automatically.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDocument everything with OpenAPI, that can be used   <a href="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/c6dfa6467cf5965f88acb219e3df6b5df68ac278543764abf5ae4a1d1c7dd020/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f6c6f676f2d6d617267696e2f6c6f676f2d7465616c2e706e67" alt="FastAPI" data-canonical-src="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/img/logo-margin/logo-teal.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a>
</p>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
    <em>FastAPI framework, high performance, easy to learn, fast to code, ready for production</em>
</p>
<p align="center" dir="auto">
<a href="https://github.com/fastapi/fastapi/actions?query=workflow%3ATest+event%3Apush+branch%3Amaster">
    <img src="https://github.com/fastapi/fastapi/actions/workflows/test.yml/badge.svg?event=push&amp;branch=master" alt="Test" style="max-width: 100%;">
</a>
<a href="https://coverage-badge.samuelcolvin.workers.dev/redirect/fastapi/fastapi" rel="nofollow">
    <img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/417c9fb0bf086f6618004816890b222c8b5c1d7922ea9b0b85b95b4c15fe9c4e/68747470733a2f2f636f7665726167652d62616467652e73616d75656c636f6c76696e2e776f726b6572732e6465762f666173746170692f666173746170692e737667" alt="Coverage" data-canonical-src="https://coverage-badge.samuelcolvin.workers.dev/fastapi/fastapi.svg" style="max-width: 100%;">
</a>
<a href="https://pypi.org/project/fastapi" rel="nofollow">
    <img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/0bed6732ebefa78b33634cf1e51d89a0f4b94baa9360da42713a6787d3c0391d/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f707970692f762f666173746170693f636f6c6f723d253233333444303538266c6162656c3d707970692532307061636b616765" alt="Package version" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/pypi/v/fastapi?color=%2334D058&amp;label=pypi%20package" style="max-width: 100%;">
</a>
<a href="https://pypi.org/project/fastapi" rel="nofollow">
    <img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/6560e9436f5127bccd57f394211bad3207d8c0400bee488324f10adfa8eb1f80/68747470733a2f2f696d672e736869656c64732e696f2f707970692f707976657273696f6e732f666173746170692e7376673f636f6c6f723d253233333444303538" alt="Supported Python versions" data-canonical-src="https://img.shields.io/pypi/pyversions/fastapi.svg?color=%2334D058" style="max-width: 100%;">
</a>
</p>
<hr>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Documentation</strong>: <a href="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com" rel="nofollow">https://fastapi.tiangolo.com</a></p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Source Code</strong>: <a href="https://github.com/fastapi/fastapi">https://github.com/fastapi/fastapi</a></p>
<hr>
<p dir="auto">FastAPI is a modern, fast (high-performance), web framework for building APIs with Python based on standard Python type hints.</p>
<p dir="auto">The key features are:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>Fast</strong>: Very high performance, on par with <strong>NodeJS</strong> and <strong>Go</strong> (thanks to Starlette and Pydantic). <a href="#performance">One of the fastest Python frameworks available</a>.</li>
<li><strong>Fast to code</strong>: Increase the speed to develop features by about 200% to 300%. *</li>
<li><strong>Fewer bugs</strong>: Reduce about 40% of human (developer) induced errors. *</li>
<li><strong>Intuitive</strong>: Great editor support. Completion everywhere. Less time debugging.</li>
<li><strong>Easy</strong>: Designed to be easy to use and learn. Less time reading docs.</li>
<li><strong>Short</strong>: Minimize code duplication. Multiple features from each parameter declaration. Fewer bugs.</li>
<li><strong>Robust</strong>: Get production-ready code. With automatic interactive documentation.</li>
<li><strong>Standards-based</strong>: Based on (and fully compatible with) the open standards for APIs: <a href="https://github.com/OAI/OpenAPI-Specification">OpenAPI</a> (previously known as Swagger) and <a href="https://json-schema.org/" rel="nofollow">JSON Schema</a>.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">* estimation based on tests conducted by an internal development team, building production applications.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><strong>Typer</strong>, the FastAPI of CLIs</h2><a id="user-content-typer-the-fastapi-of-clis" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Typer, the FastAPI of CLIs" href="#typer-the-fastapi-of-clis"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><a href="https://typer.tiangolo.com" rel="nofollow"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/bcdcde740a18c406b69fc4bfafb6fbd4c70d5e9565cfb0c8dbcf46c6d33e1ad2/68747470733a2f2f74797065722e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f6c6f676f2d6d617267696e2f6c6f676f2d6d617267696e2d766563746f722e737667" style="width: 20%; max-width: 100%;" data-canonical-src="https://typer.tiangolo.com/img/logo-margin/logo-margin-vector.svg"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">If you are building a CLI app to be used in the terminal instead of a web API, check out <a href="https://typer.tiangolo.com/" rel="nofollow"><strong>Typer</strong></a>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Typer</strong> is FastAPI's little sibling. And it's intended to be the <strong>FastAPI of CLIs</strong>. ⌨️ 🚀</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Requirements</h2><a id="user-content-requirements" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Requirements" href="#requirements"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">FastAPI stands on the shoulders of giants:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://starlette.dev/" rel="nofollow">Starlette</a> for the web parts.</li>
<li><a href="https://pydantic.dev/docs/" rel="nofollow">Pydantic</a> for the data parts.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Installation</h2><a id="user-content-installation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Installation" href="#installation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">First, <a href="https://docs.astral.sh/uv/getting-started/installation/" rel="nofollow">install <code>uv</code></a>, and then add FastAPI to your project:</p>
<div dir="auto">
<div class="highlight highlight-text-shell-session notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ uv add &quot;fastapi[standard]&quot;

---&gt; 100%"><pre>$ <span class="pl-s1">uv add <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>fastapi[standard]<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span></span>

<span class="pl-c1">---&gt; 100%</span></pre></div>
</div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note</strong>: Make sure you put <code>"fastapi[standard]"</code> in quotes to ensure it works in all terminals.</p>
<p dir="auto">If you prefer to use <code>pip</code>, install <code>fastapi[standard]</code> inside a virtual environment. See the <a href="/fastapi/fastapi/blob/master/tutorial/#install-fastapi">installation guide</a> for the alternative steps.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example</h2><a id="user-content-example" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example" href="#example"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Create it</h3><a id="user-content-create-it" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Create it" href="#create-it"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Create a file <code>main.py</code> with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from fastapi import FastAPI

app = FastAPI()


@app.get(&quot;/&quot;)
def read_root():
    return {&quot;Hello&quot;: &quot;World&quot;}


@app.get(&quot;/items/{item_id}&quot;)
def read_item(item_id: int, q: str | None = None):
    return {&quot;item_id&quot;: item_id, &quot;q&quot;: q}"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">fastapi</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">FastAPI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">app</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">FastAPI</span>()


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/"</span>)</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">read_root</span>():
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"Hello"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"World"</span>}


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/items/{item_id}"</span>)</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">read_item</span>(<span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>: <span class="pl-smi">int</span>, <span class="pl-s1">q</span>: <span class="pl-s1">str</span> <span class="pl-c1">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>):
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"item_id"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>, <span class="pl-s">"q"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">q</span>}</pre></div>
<details>
<summary>Or use <code>async def</code>...</summary>
<p dir="auto">If your code uses <code>async</code> / <code>await</code>, use <code>async def</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from fastapi import FastAPI

app = FastAPI()


@app.get(&quot;/&quot;)
async def read_root():
    return {&quot;Hello&quot;: &quot;World&quot;}


@app.get(&quot;/items/{item_id}&quot;)
async def read_item(item_id: int, q: str | None = None):
    return {&quot;item_id&quot;: item_id, &quot;q&quot;: q}"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">fastapi</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">FastAPI</span>

<span class="pl-s1">app</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">FastAPI</span>()


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/"</span>)</span>
<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">read_root</span>():
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"Hello"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"World"</span>}


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/items/{item_id}"</span>)</span>
<span class="pl-k">async</span> <span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">read_item</span>(<span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>: <span class="pl-smi">int</span>, <span class="pl-s1">q</span>: <span class="pl-s1">str</span> <span class="pl-c1">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>):
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"item_id"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>, <span class="pl-s">"q"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">q</span>}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Note</strong>:</p>
<p dir="auto">If you don't know, check the <em>"In a hurry?"</em> section about <a href="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/async/#in-a-hurry" rel="nofollow"><code>async</code> and <code>await</code> in the docs</a>.</p>
</details>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Run it</h3><a id="user-content-run-it" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Run it" href="#run-it"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Run the server with:</p>

<details>
<summary>About the command <code>fastapi dev</code>...</summary>
<p dir="auto">The command <code>fastapi dev</code> reads your <code>main.py</code> file automatically, detects the <strong>FastAPI</strong> app in it, and starts a server using <a href="https://uvicorn.dev" rel="nofollow">Uvicorn</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">By default, <code>fastapi dev</code> will start with auto-reload enabled for local development.</p>
<p dir="auto">You can read more about it in the <a href="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/fastapi-cli/" rel="nofollow">FastAPI CLI docs</a>.</p>
</details>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Check it</h3><a id="user-content-check-it" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Check it" href="#check-it"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Open your browser at <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/items/5?q=somequery" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/items/5?q=somequery</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">You will see the JSON response as:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-json notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="{&quot;item_id&quot;: 5, &quot;q&quot;: &quot;somequery&quot;}"><pre>{<span class="pl-ent">"item_id"</span>: <span class="pl-c1">5</span>, <span class="pl-ent">"q"</span>: <span class="pl-s"><span class="pl-pds">"</span>somequery<span class="pl-pds">"</span></span>}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">You already created an API that:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Receives HTTP requests in the <em>paths</em> <code>/</code> and <code>/items/{item_id}</code>.</li>
<li>Both <em>paths</em> take <code>GET</code> <em>operations</em> (also known as HTTP <em>methods</em>).</li>
<li>The <em>path</em> <code>/items/{item_id}</code> has a <em>path parameter</em> <code>item_id</code> that should be an <code>int</code>.</li>
<li>The <em>path</em> <code>/items/{item_id}</code> has an optional <code>str</code> <em>query parameter</em> <code>q</code>.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Interactive API docs</h3><a id="user-content-interactive-api-docs" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Interactive API docs" href="#interactive-api-docs"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Now go to <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto">You will see the automatic interactive API documentation (provided by <a href="https://github.com/swagger-api/swagger-ui">Swagger UI</a>):</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/c300f81580fe330c3f2cf4d3ecb4c5890f37d6be89386aa110081c708ff90469/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30312d737761676765722d75692d73696d706c652e706e67"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/c300f81580fe330c3f2cf4d3ecb4c5890f37d6be89386aa110081c708ff90469/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30312d737761676765722d75692d73696d706c652e706e67" alt="Swagger UI" data-canonical-src="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/img/index/index-01-swagger-ui-simple.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Alternative API docs</h3><a id="user-content-alternative-api-docs" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Alternative API docs" href="#alternative-api-docs"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">And now, go to <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/redoc" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/redoc</a>.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Example upgrade</h2><a id="user-content-example-upgrade" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Example upgrade" href="#example-upgrade"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Now modify the file <code>main.py</code> to receive a body from a <code>PUT</code> request.</p>
<p dir="auto">Declare the body using standard Python types, thanks to Pydantic.</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="from fastapi import FastAPI
from pydantic import BaseModel

app = FastAPI()


class Item(BaseModel):
    name: str
    price: float
    is_offer: bool | None = None


@app.get(&quot;/&quot;)
def read_root():
    return {&quot;Hello&quot;: &quot;World&quot;}


@app.get(&quot;/items/{item_id}&quot;)
def read_item(item_id: int, q: str | None = None):
    return {&quot;item_id&quot;: item_id, &quot;q&quot;: q}


@app.put(&quot;/items/{item_id}&quot;)
def update_item(item_id: int, item: Item):
    return {&quot;item_name&quot;: item.name, &quot;item_id&quot;: item_id}"><pre><span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">fastapi</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">FastAPI</span>
<span class="pl-k">from</span> <span class="pl-s1">pydantic</span> <span class="pl-k">import</span> <span class="pl-v">BaseModel</span>

<span class="pl-s1">app</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-en">FastAPI</span>()


<span class="pl-k">class</span> <span class="pl-v">Item</span>(<span class="pl-v">BaseModel</span>):
    <span class="pl-s1">name</span>: <span class="pl-smi">str</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">price</span>: <span class="pl-smi">float</span>
    <span class="pl-s1">is_offer</span>: <span class="pl-s1">bool</span> <span class="pl-c1">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/"</span>)</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">read_root</span>():
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"Hello"</span>: <span class="pl-s">"World"</span>}


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">get</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/items/{item_id}"</span>)</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">read_item</span>(<span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>: <span class="pl-smi">int</span>, <span class="pl-s1">q</span>: <span class="pl-s1">str</span> <span class="pl-c1">|</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span> <span class="pl-c1">=</span> <span class="pl-c1">None</span>):
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"item_id"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>, <span class="pl-s">"q"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">q</span>}


<span class="pl-en">@<span class="pl-s1">app</span>.<span class="pl-c1">put</span>(<span class="pl-s">"/items/{item_id}"</span>)</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">update_item</span>(<span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>: <span class="pl-smi">int</span>, <span class="pl-s1">item</span>: <span class="pl-smi">Item</span>):
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"item_name"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item</span>.<span class="pl-c1">name</span>, <span class="pl-s">"item_id"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">The <code>fastapi dev</code> server should reload automatically.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Interactive API docs upgrade</h3><a id="user-content-interactive-api-docs-upgrade" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Interactive API docs upgrade" href="#interactive-api-docs-upgrade"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Now go to <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/docs</a>.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The interactive API documentation will be automatically updated, including the new body:</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/0d6cf9164c3b2c4aef9ebe719725c5609b28a77759f7eae49436e9f6163293a9/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30332d737761676765722d30322e706e67"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/0d6cf9164c3b2c4aef9ebe719725c5609b28a77759f7eae49436e9f6163293a9/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30332d737761676765722d30322e706e67" alt="Swagger UI" data-canonical-src="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/img/index/index-03-swagger-02.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Click on the button "Try it out", it allows you to fill the parameters and directly interact with the API:</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/b0cc922a52ad995c6f91b6fe66b0302f96ca638df89bedae12f9b623f86542d2/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30342d737761676765722d30332e706e67"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/b0cc922a52ad995c6f91b6fe66b0302f96ca638df89bedae12f9b623f86542d2/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30342d737761676765722d30332e706e67" alt="Swagger UI interaction" data-canonical-src="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/img/index/index-04-swagger-03.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Then click on the "Execute" button, the user interface will communicate with your API, send the parameters, get the results and show them on the screen:</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/023db3cc6211fdea376214be08e2aadfb6d386bc2fecaa5fce32db3281ae72b7/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30352d737761676765722d30342e706e67"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/023db3cc6211fdea376214be08e2aadfb6d386bc2fecaa5fce32db3281ae72b7/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30352d737761676765722d30342e706e67" alt="Swagger UI interaction" data-canonical-src="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/img/index/index-05-swagger-04.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Alternative API docs upgrade</h3><a id="user-content-alternative-api-docs-upgrade" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Alternative API docs upgrade" href="#alternative-api-docs-upgrade"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">And now, go to <a href="http://127.0.0.1:8000/redoc" rel="nofollow">http://127.0.0.1:8000/redoc</a>.</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>The alternative documentation will also reflect the new query parameter and body:</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/ff3fbd37417a38801f8682ff0509e9f50c8303ce72275c4a2d4fb128d7dbe61f/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30362d7265646f632d30322e706e67"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/ff3fbd37417a38801f8682ff0509e9f50c8303ce72275c4a2d4fb128d7dbe61f/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f696e6465782f696e6465782d30362d7265646f632d30322e706e67" alt="ReDoc" data-canonical-src="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/img/index/index-06-redoc-02.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Recap</h3><a id="user-content-recap" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Recap" href="#recap"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">In summary, you declare <strong>once</strong> the types of parameters, body, etc. as function parameters.</p>
<p dir="auto">You do that with standard modern Python types.</p>
<p dir="auto">You don't have to learn a new syntax, the methods or classes of a specific library, etc.</p>
<p dir="auto">Just standard <strong>Python</strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">For example, for an <code>int</code>:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="item_id: int"><pre><span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>: <span class="pl-smi">int</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">or for a more complex <code>Item</code> model:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="item: Item"><pre><span class="pl-s1">item</span>: <span class="pl-smi">Item</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">...and with that single declaration you get:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Editor support, including:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Completion.</li>
<li>Type checks.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Validation of data:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Automatic and clear errors when the data is invalid.</li>
<li>Validation even for deeply nested JSON objects.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Conversion of input data: coming from the network to Python data and types. Reading from:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>JSON.</li>
<li>Path parameters.</li>
<li>Query parameters.</li>
<li>Cookies.</li>
<li>Headers.</li>
<li>Forms.</li>
<li>Files.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Conversion of output data: converting from Python data and types to network data (as JSON):
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Convert Python types (<code>str</code>, <code>int</code>, <code>float</code>, <code>bool</code>, <code>list</code>, etc).</li>
<li><code>datetime</code> objects.</li>
<li><code>UUID</code> objects.</li>
<li>Database models.</li>
<li>...and many more.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Automatic interactive API documentation, including 2 alternative user interfaces:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Swagger UI.</li>
<li>ReDoc.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<p dir="auto">Coming back to the previous code example, <strong>FastAPI</strong> will:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Validate that there is an <code>item_id</code> in the path for <code>GET</code> and <code>PUT</code> requests.</li>
<li>Validate that the <code>item_id</code> is of type <code>int</code> for <code>GET</code> and <code>PUT</code> requests.
<ul dir="auto">
<li>If it is not, the client will see a useful, clear error.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Check if there is an optional query parameter named <code>q</code> (as in <code>http://127.0.0.1:8000/items/foo?q=somequery</code>) for <code>GET</code> requests.
<ul dir="auto">
<li>As the <code>q</code> parameter is declared with <code>= None</code>, it is optional.</li>
<li>Without the <code>None</code> it would be required (as is the body in the case with <code>PUT</code>).</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>For <code>PUT</code> requests to <code>/items/{item_id}</code>, read the body as JSON:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Check that it has a required attribute <code>name</code> that should be a <code>str</code>.</li>
<li>Check that it has a required attribute <code>price</code> that has to be a <code>float</code>.</li>
<li>Check that it has an optional attribute <code>is_offer</code>, that should be a <code>bool</code>, if present.</li>
<li>All this would also work for deeply nested JSON objects.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Convert from and to JSON automatically.</li>
<li>Document everything with OpenAPI, that can be used by:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Interactive documentation systems.</li>
<li>Automatic client code generation systems, for many languages.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Provide 2 interactive documentation web interfaces directly.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<p dir="auto">We just scratched the surface, but you already get the idea of how it all works.</p>
<p dir="auto">Try changing the line with:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="    return {&quot;item_name&quot;: item.name, &quot;item_id&quot;: item_id}"><pre>    <span class="pl-k">return</span> {<span class="pl-s">"item_name"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item</span>.<span class="pl-c1">name</span>, <span class="pl-s">"item_id"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item_id</span>}</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">...from:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="        ... &quot;item_name&quot;: item.name ..."><pre>        ... <span class="pl-s">"item_name"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item</span>.<span class="pl-c1">name</span> ...</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">...to:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="        ... &quot;item_price&quot;: item.price ..."><pre>        ... <span class="pl-s">"item_price"</span>: <span class="pl-s1">item</span>.<span class="pl-c1">price</span> ...</pre></div>
<p dir="auto">...and see how your editor will auto-complete the attributes and know their types:</p>
<p dir="auto"><a target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer nofollow" href="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/d1cf69eb83a9d0b89842c210defff3e02c76643a242f95d467d2bba10bb45364/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f7673636f64652d636f6d706c6574696f6e2e706e67"><img src="https://camo.githubusercontent.com/d1cf69eb83a9d0b89842c210defff3e02c76643a242f95d467d2bba10bb45364/68747470733a2f2f666173746170692e7469616e676f6c6f2e636f6d2f696d672f7673636f64652d636f6d706c6574696f6e2e706e67" alt="editor support" data-canonical-src="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/img/vscode-completion.png" style="max-width: 100%;"></a></p>
<p dir="auto">For a more complete example including more features, see the <a href="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/tutorial/" rel="nofollow">Tutorial - User Guide</a>.</p>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Spoiler alert</strong>: the tutorial - user guide includes:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Declaration of <strong>parameters</strong> from other different places such as: <strong>headers</strong>, <strong>cookies</strong>, <strong>form fields</strong> and <strong>files</strong>.</li>
<li>How to set <strong>validation constraints</strong> such as <code>maximum_length</code> or <code>regex</code>.</li>
<li>A very powerful and easy to use <strong>Dependency Injection</strong> system.</li>
<li>Security and authentication, including support for <strong>OAuth2</strong> with <strong>JWT tokens</strong> and <strong>HTTP Basic</strong> auth.</li>
<li>More advanced (but equally easy) techniques for declaring <strong>deeply nested JSON models</strong> (thanks to Pydantic).</li>
<li><strong>GraphQL</strong> integration with <a href="https://strawberry.rocks" rel="nofollow">Strawberry</a> and other libraries.</li>
<li>Many extra features (thanks to Starlette) such as:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>WebSockets</strong></li>
<li>extremely easy tests based on HTTPX and <code>pytest</code></li>
<li><strong>CORS</strong></li>
<li><strong>Cookie Sessions</strong></li>
<li>...and more.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Deploy your app (optional)</h3><a id="user-content-deploy-your-app-optional" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deploy your app (optional)" href="#deploy-your-app-optional"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You can optionally deploy your FastAPI app to <a href="https://fastapicloud.com" rel="nofollow">FastAPI Cloud</a> with a single command. 🚀</p>
<div dir="auto">
<div class="highlight highlight-text-shell-session notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="$ uv run fastapi deploy

Deploying to FastAPI Cloud...

✅ Deployment successful!

🐔 Ready the chicken! Your app is ready at https://myapp.fastapicloud.dev"><pre>$ <span class="pl-s1">uv run fastapi deploy</span>

<span class="pl-c1">Deploying to FastAPI Cloud...</span>

<span class="pl-c1">✅ Deployment successful!</span>

<span class="pl-c1">🐔 Ready the chicken! Your app is ready at https://myapp.fastapicloud.dev</span></pre></div>
</div>
<p dir="auto">The CLI will automatically detect your FastAPI application and deploy it to the cloud. If you are not logged in, your browser will open to complete the authentication process.</p>
<p dir="auto">That's it! Now you can access your app at that URL. ✨</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">About FastAPI Cloud</h4><a id="user-content-about-fastapi-cloud" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: About FastAPI Cloud" href="#about-fastapi-cloud"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto"><strong><a href="https://fastapicloud.com" rel="nofollow">FastAPI Cloud</a></strong> is built by the same author and team behind <strong>FastAPI</strong>.</p>
<p dir="auto">It streamlines the process of <strong>building</strong>, <strong>deploying</strong>, and <strong>accessing</strong> an API with minimal effort.</p>
<p dir="auto">It brings the same <strong>developer experience</strong> of building apps with FastAPI to <strong>deploying</strong> them to the cloud. 🎉</p>
<p dir="auto">FastAPI Cloud is the primary sponsor and funding provider for the <em>FastAPI and friends</em> open source projects. ✨</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h4 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Deploy to other cloud providers</h4><a id="user-content-deploy-to-other-cloud-providers" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Deploy to other cloud providers" href="#deploy-to-other-cloud-providers"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">FastAPI is open source and based on standards. You can deploy FastAPI apps to any cloud provider you choose.</p>
<p dir="auto">Follow your cloud provider's guides to deploy FastAPI apps with them. 🤓</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Performance</h2><a id="user-content-performance" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Performance" href="#performance"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Independent TechEmpower benchmarks show <strong>FastAPI</strong> applications running under Uvicorn as <a href="https://www.techempower.com/benchmarks/#section=test&amp;runid=7464e520-0dc2-473d-bd34-dbdfd7e85911&amp;hw=ph&amp;test=query&amp;l=zijzen-7" rel="nofollow">one of the fastest Python frameworks available</a>, only below Starlette and Uvicorn themselves (used internally by FastAPI). (*)</p>
<p dir="auto">To understand more about it, see the section <a href="https://fastapi.tiangolo.com/benchmarks/" rel="nofollow">Benchmarks</a>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Dependencies</h2><a id="user-content-dependencies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Dependencies" href="#dependencies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">FastAPI depends on Pydantic and Starlette.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto"><code>standard</code> Dependencies</h3><a id="user-content-standard-dependencies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: standard Dependencies" href="#standard-dependencies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When you install FastAPI with <code>uv add "fastapi[standard]"</code> it comes with the <code>standard</code> group of optional dependencies:</p>
<p dir="auto">Used by Pydantic:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/JoshData/python-email-validator"><code>email-validator</code></a> - for email validation.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Used by Starlette:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://www.python-httpx.org" rel="nofollow"><code>httpx</code></a> - Required if you want to use the <code>TestClient</code>.</li>
<li><a href="https://jinja.palletsprojects.com" rel="nofollow"><code>jinja2</code></a> - Required if you want to use the default template configuration.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/Kludex/python-multipart"><code>python-multipart</code></a> - Required if you want to support form "parsing", with <code>request.form()</code>.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Used by FastAPI:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://uvicorn.dev" rel="nofollow"><code>uvicorn</code></a> - for the server that loads and serves your application. This includes <code>uvicorn[standard]</code>, which includes some dependencies (e.g. <code>uvloop</code>) needed for high performance serving.</li>
<li><code>fastapi-cli[standard]</code> - to provide the <code>fastapi</code> command.
<ul dir="auto">
<li>This includes <code>fastapi-cloud-cli</code>, which allows you to deploy your FastAPI application to <a href="https://fastapicloud.com" rel="nofollow">FastAPI Cloud</a>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Without <code>standard</code> Dependencies</h3><a id="user-content-without-standard-dependencies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Without standard Dependencies" href="#without-standard-dependencies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you don't want to include the <code>standard</code> optional dependencies, you can install with <code>uv add fastapi</code> instead of <code>uv add "fastapi[standard]"</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Without <code>fastapi-cloud-cli</code></h3><a id="user-content-without-fastapi-cloud-cli" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Without fastapi-cloud-cli" href="#without-fastapi-cloud-cli"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">If you want to install FastAPI with the standard dependencies but without the <code>fastapi-cloud-cli</code>, you can install with <code>uv add "fastapi[standard-no-fastapi-cloud-cli]"</code>.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Additional Optional Dependencies</h3><a id="user-content-additional-optional-dependencies" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Additional Optional Dependencies" href="#additional-optional-dependencies"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">There are some additional dependencies you might want to install.</p>
<p dir="auto">Additional optional Pydantic dependencies:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://pydantic.dev/docs/validation/latest/concepts/pydantic_settings/" rel="nofollow"><code>pydantic-settings</code></a> - for settings management.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/pydantic/pydantic-extra-types"><code>pydantic-extra-types</code></a> - for extra types to be used with Pydantic.</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Additional optional FastAPI dependencies:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><a href="https://github.com/ijl/orjson"><code>orjson</code></a> - Required if you want to use <code>ORJSONResponse</code>.</li>
<li><a href="https://github.com/ultrajson/ultrajson"><code>ujson</code></a> - Required if you want to use <code>UJSONResponse</code>.</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">License</h2><a id="user-content-license" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: License" href="#license"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
